# Postgraduate Fees

Filter the fees below by academic year, school, course name or course code. The annual tuition fees listed apply to new students entering in the specified academic year.

To find out information about a course you are interested in studying, [search by course name here](https://www.tcd.ie/courses/postgraduate/) .

If you have a query, please contact Academic Registry through [AskTCD](https://ask.tcd.ie/portal) using the 'Log an Academic Registry Enquiry' button.

Please note:

- The Tuition fees noted in the below fee schedule relates to the first year of the course, not the fee for entire course duration.
- Please be aware that fees are subject to change each year in line with college policy.
- Fees for continuing students may be subject to a [percentage increase](https://www.tcd.ie/academicregistry/fees-and-payments/annual-fee-rate/) each year in line with College Fee Certainty regulation.
- In addition to [Tuition Fees and the Student Contribution Fee](https://www.tcd.ie/academicregistry/fees-and-payments/what-are-my-fees/) (Student Contribution Fee for undergraduate EU students only), students are required to pay a Student Levies and Charges fee annually.
- Please note that Trinity **cannot facilitate any payment plan proposals/requests** outside the two or three instalment payment options available.
